# Graph Report - batch-clone  (2026-11-14)

## Corpus Check
- 4734 files · ~10,835,752 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 108 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 33, .css 16, .entitlements 11)

## Summary
- 65891 nodes · 190216 edges · 1344 communities (1217 shown, 127 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8026 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e31941c8`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
